## 1. Strongest proved result

Canonical Batch-A / 4th-triangle pair on one fixed input C, in the paper-faithful ROOTED SPECTRAL normalization (IUT III Rem 3.1.1(ii); Prop 3.9(i); IV Prop 1.4(i)):

- `S3131CanonicalRootedQC` = Remark 3.1.1(ii)-weighted procession-normalized log-volume of the rooted spectral q-pilot region `qPilotPaperRegion` at `rootHalfCoefficient = c_norm/(2l)` (c_norm = Q_p-spectral qVFamily coefficient). By construction this is the paper q-pilot readout `-|log q|`; scalar numeric equality is NOT claimed (needs the normalized 2l-th root supply below).
- `S3131CanonicalRootedHC` = same weights · rooted Theta-hull log-volume at the same coefficient.
- `S3131CanonicalRootedQC_le_HC` : **UNCONDITIONAL signed comparison** `Q_C <= H_C`, i.e. `-|log q| <= -|log Theta|` (IUT III Cor 3.12, corpus:10352).
- `S3131CanonicalRootedMagnitude` : `|log Theta| <= |log q|`.
- Files: `tmp/sheaf/xif/paper/S3131CanonicalRootedQCActual.lean` (EXIT=0 SIZE=4055); probe `S3131CanonicalRootedProbeActual` (EXIT=0, axioms trio).

Also proved (task-13): paper coefficient is the Q_p-spectral `c_norm = order/e` (tree theorem S826 under the normalized-norm relation); the tree S805/S779 `paperRoot = order/(2l)` equals `e * rootHalfCoefficient`; the old containment comparison `paperRoot <= c_norm` is equivalent to the local ramification bound `e <= 2l` and can fail — corrected coefficient adopted. Native identification gap `QVFamNormalizedCoefficientGap` is equivalent to `e * L = 1` with `L = log(residueNormBase)/log p` (not a general identity).

### A1. `scaling := 1` provenance and fidelity (specific task-69 investigation)
- PROVENANCE: `sourceHaarActualData` is a deliberate model CONSTRUCTION (LeanFormal/IUT/IUTIII/Corollary312/StepXI/HolomorphicHull/SourceUnitRegionHaarDataActual.lean:416-430): unit region := actual source holomorphic hull, scaling := 1, degreeBound := 0; `hull_subset_scaledRegionImage` is then trivially `x = 1*x`, and the degree bounds come from the nonpositive value monoid. It is a **normalization/measure-choice**, not an arbitrary placeholder.
- IS IT EXPLOITED BY THEOREMS? NO for card (2): `sourceHaarActual_scalingLogDegree_zero` (tmp/sheaf/xif/paper/S3135ScalingActualModelActual.lean:71) is proved for EVERY `D : SourceUnitRegionHaarData` on the concrete model — it uses only `D.hull_subset_scaledRegionImage`, that the monoid unit lies in the actual hull, nonpositive log-degrees, and logDegree multiplicativity. It does NOT assume `D.scaling = 1`. Hence the concrete `S3135ScalingLogDegreeZero` card is genuinely proved for all concrete-model Haar data.
- HOWEVER the concrete `hb`/paper-lambda proof INSTANTIATES `sourceHaarActualData`, i.e. the `scaling := 1` instance; its set-equality step `mu(lambda*O)=mu(O)` (S3135PaperLambdaConcreteActual: s3135_concrete_haar_image_eq with z=z'=1) is the TRIVIAL normalization instance. It is not evidence for the paper's nontrivial procession scaling. Additionally abc's finding: the Example36 shells use `(logShellScale)^{-1}`, which has norm p and is NOT a `Z_p` unit scalar; a general paper lambda must be a genuine unit.
- RISK ASSESSMENT: **trivialization risk present but bounded** — (a) the card-level F4(b) theorem is not vacuous (holds for all concrete D); (b) the paper's nontrivial normalization/unit-scalar requirement is NOT discharged by the concrete model; it remains the flagged paper input `S3135CompletionBaseFieldUnitScalar` (minimal Prop, III Prop 3.9(i)/Rem 3.9.5(i)). If `scaling := 1` were presented as establishing the paper's F4(b) for the actual nontrivial data, that would be an input error — do not do so.
- RECOMMENDATION: (i) keep `scaling := 1` only as the concrete normalized-model instance and cite it as such; (ii) do NOT promote gate-v4 on the strength of the concrete model; keep `S3135CompletionBaseFieldUnitScalar` as a FLAGGED paper-defined input in the acceptance record; (iii) if the paper's procession scaling is required nontrivially, the concrete model must be supplemented by an actual nontrivial-scaling Haar datum (new exact missing if unavailable) — this is consistent with the user's warning on paper input errors.

### A2. VERDICT
**NO CIRCULARITY FOUND**: no audited residual/input definition references `ABCConjecture`, `Cor312Conclusion`, the gate theorems, or downstream results; hP is only a hypothesis. **TWO FLAGGED INPUT ISSUES** (input-authorization, not circularity): (1) concrete normalized model `scaling := 1` is a trivial normalization instance — its F4(b) proof does not certify the paper's nontrivial procession scaling (the card itself is proved for all concrete D, so not vacuous); (2) abstract-D F4(b) requires the flagged paper input `S3135CompletionBaseFieldUnitScalar` (and general lambda must be a genuine p-adic unit). Recommendation: acceptance record states no circularity + these two input flags; gate-v4 promotion deferred; combined registry reopen (M2a CLOSED + audit verdict citation) only after Lead confirms.

### A12. F4.a `S3135ValueGroupDegreeCompatibility` — proof analysis: SOLVABLE ONLY WITH NEW SOURCE DATA (not derivable from current `SourceImageRealization`)
Definition (abc `S3135ValueGroupCompatibilityActual:53`): `∀ r : Multiplicative ℝ, ofAdd (I.logDegreeAt 0 0 v (R.posRealAction r)) = globalDegree (normalizedRealifiedPrimePowerHom vp r)`; with `normalizedRealifiedPrimePowerHom_degree` (qpilot) the RHS is `r`, so compatibility ⇔ `logDegreeAt ∘ posRealAction = ofAdd` on all of `Multiplicative ℝ`.
WHY NOT DERIVABLE: `SourceImageRealization` (SourceImageHull.lean:161-169) records only (i) `posRealAction : Multiplicative ℝ →* I.ValueMonoidAt 0 0 v` (an ARBITRARY monoid hom) and (ii) `degree_of_q` at the SINGLE point `log q` (and the docstring's 'x ↦ x' is not a field). Let `h := logDegreeAt ∘ posRealAction : Multiplicative ℝ →* Multiplicative ℝ`. The structure constrains `h` only at `ofAdd (log q)`; endomorphisms of the divisible group `Multiplicative ℝ` include all ℚ-linear maps `r ↦ c·r` with `c ∈ ℚ` (and wilder ones), so `degree_of_q` (log q ≠ 0) pins `c = 1` for the ℚ-linear family but cannot rule out non-ℚ-linear monoid homs. No theorem in the tree forces `h = id`.
REDUCED MISSING (new datum, paper-faithful): the full degree faithfulness of the source realization — `logDegreeAt ∘ posRealAction = ofAdd` as monoid homs (IUT III Remark 3.9.4(iv): the value-group embedding `x ↦ x` IS the log parameter; IUT II section 1; [AbsTopIII] Prop 5.8(ii)). This is exactly the paper's value-group/realified-Frobenioid identification, i.e. F4.a is NOT removable by the current abstract record — it needs either (a) this new source datum in `SourceImageRealization`, or (b) the from-scratch Example 3.6/Gaussian model (task-72) which can define `posRealAction` with degree-faithfulness built in (then F4.a closes for THAT model, not for arbitrary abstract D).
COROLLARY for general residual count: F4.a remains a genuine source identification; the general-theorem residual set is still {F4.a, F4.b nontrivial paper input, F4.c card P}. F4.a closure via task-72 concrete model would be a MODEL closure (documented as such), not the abstract-D closure.
